- **web-research-cascade**: A robust web-fetch cascade so that blocked primary sources still get read instead of being silently skipped. Use this skill whenever a URL is fetched, a page needs to be researched, or WebFetch/WebSearch returns a 403/401/429/"unable to fetch"/empty body/CAPTCHA.
